# Master Course-Level Tech Learning Catalogue for Bhopal (2026)

This third edition converts the earlier 260-record resource archive into an **881-row course/module catalogue**. Catalogue hubs are no longer treated as though they were single courses: certifications, university subjects, micro-courses, language tracks, security topics, book-courses, practice tracks and selected lesson series are independently classified. Every row carries a deep or best-available official URL, access boundary, language/Hindi field, workload estimate, prerequisites, scope, tier rationale and September 2026 status.

## Method and classification rules

- **Inventory first:** all 260 v2 records were normalized, then 60 likely hubs were identified from their type/name. Existing single-course and high-value reference records were preserved; child courses were added as separate records.
- **Expansion:** required catalogues were queried in parallel, official curriculum/course pages were preferred, and results were checkpointed as JSONL. The CSV is the canonical deduplicated row set.
- **Tiering:** *Newbie* assumes no prior subject knowledge; *Apprentice* develops independent routine use; *Adept* requires prior programming/mathematics/domain foundations and handles integrated projects; *Master* covers advanced theory, systems depth, research-level material or difficult capstones. A course can legitimately span adjacent tiers.
- **Cost:** `free` means learning materials are available without payment; `free-to-audit` separates free learning from paid grading/certification; `freemium` means only a stated subset or allocation is free; `free-with-account` requires registration; and `eligibility-based` depends on programme rules.
- **Verification:** 760 rows carry active/checked status; 84 official links were blocked to automated reading by robots/JavaScript/IP controls; and 37 links remain explicitly flagged as unresolved or potentially moved. A fetch restriction is not treated as proof of retirement.

## Recommended primary-plus-alternative picks

These picks turn the catalogue into a decision aid. They prioritize fetched/official sources, coherent curricula, free learning access, mobile-friendly reading/video where practical, and a second option with a different teaching style. Where a track lacks a standalone Master course, the strongest Adept resource is deliberately paired with a capstone-oriented alternative.

## Data and update protocol

- The companion CSV has exactly one row per deduplicated course/module identity and is the preferred input for filtering, local advising, translation queues, or future refreshes.
- Raw JSONL preserves the same normalized findings, hub inventory, wide-search results, link-check outputs and error evidence. Future updates should re-run URL checks, replace moved deep links, and append newly launched individual courses rather than collapsing them back into platform hubs.
- `last_verified_status` must remain visible in learner-facing derivatives so that dynamic catalogues, archived university terms, free-credit allocations and optional certificate fees are not mistaken for permanent guarantees.
